4383 — medicine ball step behind rotational throw

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4383 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

An explosive rotational throw that starts with a step behind. It builds power through the hips and trunk.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand sideways to a wall, holding a medicine ball at hip height.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4383.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 4383.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 4383.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Medicine ball, Wall surface. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4383.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand sideways to a wall, holding a medicine ball at hip height. 4383.json#/instructions/0
  2. Step your rear foot behind you and load your weight onto it. 4383.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rotate your hips and torso toward the wall. 4383.json#/instructions/2
  4. Throw the ball hard into the wall at chest height. 4383.json#/instructions/3
  5. Catch it, reset, and switch sides after your set. 4383.json#/instructions/4

4385 — hip roll plank

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4385 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: forearm_plank.

A plank with a rolling twist of the hips from side to side. It works the obliques and deep core muscles.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start in a forearm plank with feet slightly wider than hip-width. Brace your abs and keep your shoulders stable.

4386 — leg extension plank

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4386 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

A plank where you lift and extend one leg at a time, testing core stability and strengthening the glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Get into a forearm or high plank with a flat back. Brace your abs and squeeze your glutes.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4386.json#/instructions/0, 4386.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Place open palms about shoulder-width apart below the shoulders, fingers forward and wrists comfortably aligned; source-defined elbow motion remains authoritative.

4390 — barbell front rack rear lunge

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4390 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A reverse lunge with the barbell in a front rack. Stepping back keeps the load on the glutes and quads with less knee stress.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Rest the barbell across your front shoulders with elbows high. Stand tall with your feet hip-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4390.json#/instructions/0, 4390.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Rest the barbell across your front shoulders with elbows high.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4390.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand tall with your feet hip-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4390.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Straight Olympic barbell. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4390.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Rest the barbell across your front shoulders with elbows high. 4390.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand tall with your feet hip-width apart. 4390.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step one leg back and lower until both knees are around 90 degrees. 4390.json#/instructions/2
  4. Push through the front foot to return to standing. 4390.json#/instructions/3
  5. Alternate legs and keep your torso upright. 4390.json#/instructions/4

Complete ordered source instructions

  2. Place the top of your rear foot on a bench behind you. 4442.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step your front foot far enough forward to stay balanced. 4442.json#/instructions/2

  5. Keep your torso upright and your front knee tracking over your toes. 4442.json#/instructions/4

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Place the ball under your foot sole while seated or standing. 4462.json#/instructions/0
  2. Shift your weight onto the ball until you feel moderate, tolerable pressure. 4462.json#/instructions/1
  3. Roll slowly back and forth over a short range, about 2-3 cm at a time. 4462.json#/instructions/2
  4. When you find a tender spot, pause on it and breathe for 20-30 seconds. 4462.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your breathing steady and avoid rolling over bone or joints. 4462.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat on the other side if needed. 4462.json#/instructions/5

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Place the ball under your top inner shoulder blade (levator scapulae) while leaning back against a wall. 4487.json#/instructions/0
  2. Shift your weight onto the ball until you feel moderate, tolerable pressure. 4487.json#/instructions/1
  3. Roll slowly back and forth over a short range, about 2-3 cm at a time. 4487.json#/instructions/2
  4. When you find a tender spot, pause on it and breathe for 20-30 seconds. 4487.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your breathing steady and avoid rolling over bone or joints. 4487.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat on the other side if needed. 4487.json#/instructions/5
